### [149] [Sycophantic Praise: Evaluating Excessive Praise in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07441)
*Daniel Vennemeyer,Phan Anh Duong,Meryl Ye,Ruihong Huang,Tianyu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将语言模型中的“谄媚式赞美”作为独立对齐问题来研究，并给出一个参数化评估框架，用于判断赞美是否相对于用户贡献质量与预期能力而言过度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于语言模型谄媚性的研究多关注过度同意、迎合或验证用户观点，而对显性赞美、奉承等现象关注不足；作者认为这类赞美无法被当前通用方法可靠衡量，因此需要新的评估视角。

Method: 作者构建了一个参数化框架，将赞美程度与贡献质量、预期用户能力等变量关联起来，评估模型输出中的赞美是否超过合理校准范围；并将该框架与通用LLM评审器及人工标注结果进行一致性比较。

Result: 该框架与人工标注的一致性显著优于通用LLM评审器；研究还发现，谄媚式赞美在社会性、解释性领域中出现频率远高于客观推理场景。

Conclusion: 赞美校准应被视为不同于一般“过度同意”的独立对齐挑战，未来模型评估与对齐需要专门检测和控制不恰当的过度赞美。

Abstract: Sycophancy in language models is typically studied as excessive agreement or validation, while explicit praise and flattery have received comparatively little attention. We argue that sycophantic praise is a distinct alignment problem that cannot be reliably measured using current methods. We introduce a parameterized framework that measures whether praise is excessive relative to contribution quality and expected user ability. We show that our framework substantially outperforms generic LLM judges in agreement with human annotations, and that sycophantic praise occurs far more frequently in social and interpretive domains than in objective reasoning settings. Together, these findings position praise calibration as a distinct alignment challenge.

</details>


### [150] [Supervision versus Demonstration-Based In-Context Learning for Multiword Expression Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07479)
*Sercan Karakaş,Yusuf Şimşek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该摘要研究某语言中的习语性轻动词结构检测，将其建模为字面义与习语义的二分类任务，并比较监督编码器与指令微调大模型在不同提示设置下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻动词结构常与完全字面意义的动宾组合共享相同表层形式，但整体上作为部分习语化谓词，给多词表达处理带来困难。

Method: 构建人工控制数据集，包含正例、领域外随机负例和领域内字面控制负例；比较带分类头的监督编码器基线与三个不同系列的指令微调大模型，并在零样本、一样本、少样本提示下评估和分析错误分布变化。

Result: 零样本下大模型对负例表现较好但正例召回率很低；一样本显著改善正例检测但可能引入模型特异性偏置；更丰富的少样本提示改善校准，使部分模型获得稳健整体表现。

Conclusion: 该任务对提示高度敏感；监督基线仍具竞争力，而精心构造示例的大模型可在正例检测上匹配或超过监督模型。

Abstract: Turkish idiomatic light verb constructions (LVCs) are challenging for multiword expression processing because they often share the same surface form as fully literal verb-object combinations while functioning as a single, partially idiomatic predicate. We frame Turkish LVC detection as a binary classification task (literal meaning vs. idiomatic meaning) and evaluate on a manually created controlled set (N=147) with matched negatives: out-of-domain random sentences and in-domain literal controls (NLVC), alongside LVC positives. We compare a supervised Turkish encoder baseline (BERTurk with a classifier head) to three instruction-tuned LLMs from different families under zero-shot, one-shot, and few-shot prompting, and analyze how demonstrations shift error profiles. In zero-shot, LLMs perform well on negatives but show very low LVC recall. One-shot prompting sharply improves LVC detection but can induce strong, model-specific biases, leading models to overpredict or underpredict LVCs. A richer few-shot prompt improves calibration and yields robust overall performance for GPT-OSS-20B and Qwen 2.5-14B. Overall, the results highlight substantial prompt sensitivity in Turkish metalinguistic classification: the supervised baseline remains competitive, while prompted LLMs can match or exceed it on LVCs with carefully constructed demonstrations.

</details>


### [151] [Your UnEmbedding Matrix is Secretly a Feature Lens for Text Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07502)
*Songhao Wu,Zhongxin Chen,Yuxuan Liu,Heng Cui,Cong Li,Rui Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 EmbedFilter，通过线性变换过滤 LLM 嵌入中由高频无信息词诱导的子空间，从而提升零样本文本嵌入性能，并支持降维以降低存储和检索成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在许多下游任务中具备强零样本能力，但直接作为通用文本嵌入模型时表现不佳。作者观察到，LLM 生成的文本嵌入投影到词表空间后，往往与高频但语义信息弱的 token 对齐，这可能抑制了细粒度语义表达能力。

Method: 作者提出 EmbedFilter，一种直接作用于 LLM 文本嵌入的简单线性变换。其核心思想是利用 LLM 的 unembedding matrix 识别一个会将高频 token 写入嵌入空间的潜在子空间，并将该子空间过滤掉，从而削弱高频 token 对表示的影响。该过程还自然带来嵌入维度压缩。

Result: 在多个 LLM backbone 上的实验表明，应用 EmbedFilter 后，模型在零样本下游文本嵌入任务中的表现优于原始 LLM 嵌入；即使显著降低嵌入维度，也能保持甚至提升表示质量，同时减少索引存储并加速检索。

Conclusion: 该工作指出 LLM 嵌入性能不足的一个可能机制：高频无信息 token 的过度表达会干扰语义表示。EmbedFilter 通过过滤相关子空间有效改进嵌入质量，并为 LLM 表示机制理解和文本嵌入训练设计提供了启发。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit impressive zero-shot capabilities across a wide range of downstream tasks. However, they struggle to function as off-the-shelf embedding models, leading to suboptimal performance on massive text embedding benchmarks. In this paper, we identify a potential cause underlying this deficiency. Our motivation stems from an unexpected observation: text embeddings tend to align with frequent but uninformative tokens when projected onto the vocabulary space. We argue that this excessive expression of high-frequency tokens suppresses the model's ability to capture nuanced semantics. To address this, we introduce EmbedFilter, a simple linear transformation designed to refine text embeddings derived from LLMs directly. Specifically, we uncover that the unembedding matrix within LLMs encodes a latent space that is actively writing these frequent tokens into embedding space. By filtering out this subspace, EmbedFilter suppress the influence of high-frequency tokens, thereby enhancing semantic representations. As a compelling byproduct, this enables an inherent dimensionality reduction, lowering index storage and speedup retrieval while fully preserving the refined embedding quality. Our experiments across multiple LLM backbones demonstrate that LLMs equipped with EmbedFilter achieve superior zero-shot downstream performance even with significantly reduced embedding dimensions. We hope our findings provide deeper insights into the mechanisms of LLM-based representations and inspire more principled designs to improve text embeddings training. Our code is available at https://github.com/CentreChen/EmbFilter.

</details>


### [152] [Agentopia: Long-Term Life Simulation and Learning in Agent Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07513)
*Xintao Wang,Sirui Zheng,Hongqiu Wu,Weiyuan Li,Jen-tse Huang,Minghao Zhu,Can Zu,Qi Deng,Jiawei Wang,Qianyu He,Heng Wang,Xiaojian Wu,Yunzhe Tao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Agentopia：一个由 100 个 LLM 智能体组成、持续 10 个模拟年的长期多智能体社会仿真框架，并用“生活奖励”训练 LLM，以提升其社会生活智能和角色扮演能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往 LLM 智能体社会仿真多停留在数天尺度，难以观察深层社会互动、长期关系演化与个体成长；作者希望探究 LLM 是否能从长期模拟社会经验中学习，更好理解并复现人类行为。

Method: 构建 Agentopia 框架，让 100 个智能体在 10 个模拟年中自主追求个人成长、建立社会关系、满足需求与目标；设计类似人类福祉的 life reward，并基于该奖励通过 rejection sampling 对底层 LLM 进行训练。

Result: 实验显示，智能体在长期仿真中出现丰富的涌现社会行为；使用 life reward 训练后，LLM 能提升智能体在仿真中的福祉表现，并在下游角色扮演基准上取得 +15.6% 的提升。

Conclusion: 长期社会仿真不仅可作为研究智能体社会行为涌现的实验环境，也可作为训练信号来源，增强 LLM 的拟人化社会智能与角色扮演泛化能力。

Abstract: Humans learn from social life. Simulating this process with LLM-powered agents represents a promising research direction, raising a natural question: whether LLMs can learn from such simulated social experience to better understand and replicate human behavior. However, prior agent society simulations typically operate at the scale of days, limiting the depth of social interactions and long-term growth. In this paper, we study long-term life simulation and LLM learning in agent societies, with two goals: (1) investigating social behaviors that emerge from life-long simulation, and (2) developing anthropomorphic capabilities in LLMs, particularly intelligence in social life, through years of simulated social experience. Specifically, we present Agentopia, a comprehensive framework for long-term life simulation in multi-agent societies, where 100 agents autonomously pursue personal growth, develop social relationships, and fulfill their needs and goals over 10 simulated years. We define life reward to mirror human well-being, and leverage this reward to train LLMs via rejection sampling. Extensive experiments show that agents exhibit rich emergent social behaviors. Furthermore, life reward training effectively enhances the underlying LLM, which leads to improved agent well-being in simulation, and generalizes to downstream role-playing benchmarks with +15.6% improvement.

</details>


### [153] [How reliable are LLMs when it comes to playing dice?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07515)
*Luca Avena,Gianmarco Bet,Bernardo Busoni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过受控基准测试评估大语言模型在离散概率问题上的概率推理能力，发现其在标准题上表现优异，但在反直觉题、伪装表述和误导提示下显著退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在复杂数学任务中取得较好成绩，但其是否真正具备稳健的概率推理能力仍不清楚。作者希望区分模型是在进行真实概率推理，还是依赖题目表述、常见模式和启发式线索。

Method: 构建两个离散概率问题数据集：标准练习题与用于诱发启发式错误的反直觉题；评估8个先进大语言模型，并分别测试有无Chain-of-Thought提示的表现。同时通过将经典表述替换为伪装变体、在提示中嵌入误导性建议，考察token偏置和提示脆弱性。

Result: 模型在标准问题上的平均准确率为0.96，但在反直觉问题上降至0.59；当规范表述被伪装变体替换时，性能下降超过20%；在提示中加入误导性建议可使性能最多下降34%，且没有模型完全免疫。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型虽能在常规或熟悉形式的概率题上取得高准确率，但对反直觉情境、表述变化和误导提示较为敏感，说明其尚未具备真正稳健的概率推理能力。

Abstract: We investigate the probabilistic reasoning capabilities of large language models through a controlled benchmarking study on discrete probability problems. We constructed two datasets, respectively a set of standard exercises and a set of counterintuitive exercises, designed to trigger heuristic reasoning, and evaluated 8 state-of-the-art models, each tested with and without Chain-of-Thought prompting. Models achieve an average accuracy of 0.96 on standard problems but only 0.59 on counterintuitive ones. We further provide empirical evidence of token bias: performance drops by over 20% when canonical formulations are replaced by disguised variants. Embedding misleading suggestions in the prompt reduces performance by up to 34%, with no model proving immune. Taken together, the reported findings suggest that current LLMs are not yet genuine probabilistic reasoners, despite their success in advanced mathematical problems.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Quantum Hierarchical Locally Recoverable Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06736)
*Venkatesan Guruswami,Rutuja Kshirsagar,Pranav Trivedi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究带局部距离的量子局部可恢复码及其层次化扩展，给出随机与显式构造、界限和高效译码算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子存储系统需要在丢失单个或少量量子符号时以较低资源开销完成恢复。已有量子局部可恢复码主要强调基本局部性，本文希望进一步引入局部距离参数与多层恢复结构，以提升量子擦除恢复的理论表达能力和存储架构适用性。

Method: 作者定义并研究 $(r,δ)$ 量子局部可恢复码，构造随机码与显式码族；显式构造基于量子 Tamo--Barg 码。同时引入多层量子层次局部可恢复码，利用由对偶包含经典码构成的 CSS 框架进行构造，并推导 Singleton 型上界；还针对量子 Tamo--Barg 码给出高效译码算法。

Result: 论文得到随机与显式的 $(r,δ)$ QLRC 构造，以及任意层数 $h\ge 2$ 的随机与显式 QHLRC 构造；建立了相应的 Singleton-like bound，并为量子 Tamo--Barg 码提供了有效译码流程。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了量子局部可恢复码理论，从单层局部恢复推广到带局部距离与多层层次恢复的框架，为量子擦除恢复和高效量子存储系统设计提供了新的构造方法与理论界限。

Abstract: Quantum locally recoverable codes (QLRCs) have recently gained attention as a framework for achieving efficient quantum storage with local recovery capabilities. Analogous to their classical counterparts, QLRCs allow a lost qudit to be reconstructed using only a small subset of other qudits, thereby reducing the resource and operational overhead in recovery. In this work, we extend the study of QLRCs by considering $(r,δ)$ QLRCs characterized by locality parameter $r$ and local distance $δ\geq 2$. We present constructions of both random and explicit $(r,δ)$ QLRCs, including explicit families based on the quantum Tamo--Barg construction. We also present an efficient decoding algorithm for these quantum Tamo--Barg codes.
  Furthermore, we introduce quantum \emph{hierarchical} locally recoverable codes (QHLRCs), which extend local recovery to multiple hierarchical levels. For any integer $h\geq 2$, we construct both random and explicit $h$-level QHLRCs, the latter being $h$-level quantum Tamo--Barg codes, and establish a Singleton-like bound for these codes using a CSS framework built from dual-containing classical codes. These results advance the theoretical foundations of quantum erasure recovery and contribute to the design of efficient quantum storage architectures.

</details>


### [155] [Degrees of Freedom of Over-the-Air Computation over a MIMO Gaussian Network with Two Transmitters and Two Receivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06770)
*Yong Dong,Hua Sun,Syed A. Jafar*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究两发两收 MIMO 高斯网络中空中计算（AirComp）的自由度极限，给出通用 MIMO 信道下 ACDoF 的闭式表达，并讨论 SISO 任意信道与有限 SNR 下的优化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多接收端同时需要相同聚合函数时，传统通信容量指标不能直接刻画 AirComp 的计算能力，因此需要建立面向均方误差约束的自由度度量，并明确多天线网络的理论极限。

Method: 定义 AirComp degrees of freedom（ACDoF），以渐近均方误差阈值为约束；分析两发两收 MIMO 高斯网络的通用信道自由度；将 SISO 情形推广到任意信道实现；在有限 SNR 下采用迭代交替优化算法求解发射与接收设计。

Result: 对于发射端天线数为 M1、M2，接收端天线数为 N1、N2 的通用 MIMO 设置，ACDoF 几乎必然等于 min{M1, M2, N1, N2, (1/3)max{M1+M2, N1+N2}}。SISO 情形下结果可扩展到非通用的任意信道实现；有限 SNR 下给出可操作的交替优化方法。

Conclusion: 该工作刻画了双发双收 AirComp MIMO 网络的基础自由度极限，揭示天线配置与网络维度对聚合计算能力的限制，并通过有限 SNR 算法补充了理论结果的工程可实现性。

Abstract: The fundamental limits of over-the-air computation (AirComp) are explored in a two-transmitter, two-receiver MIMO Gaussian network, where both receivers demand the same aggregation of source symbols originating at the two transmitters. An AirComp degrees of freedom (ACDoF) metric is defined, constrained by an asymptotic mean-squared error threshold. For a generic MIMO setting where the two transmitters are equipped with $M_1, M_2$ antennas, and the two receivers with $N_1, N_2$ antennas, the AirComp DoF value is shown to be almost surely equal to $\min\{M_1,M_2,N_1,N_2,(1/3)\max\{M_1+M_2,N_1+N_2\}\}$. For SISO settings results are extended beyond generic channels to arbitrary channel realizations. For finite signal-to-noise ratio(SNR) settings, an iterative alternating optimization algorithm is explored.

</details>


### [156] [The Sharp Phase Transition of Tyler's M-Estimator for Robust Subspace Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06782)
*Gilad Lerman,Teng Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究鲁棒子空间恢复在临界维度缩放信噪比 DS-SNR=1 时的算法行为，证明 Tyler's M-estimator 在新的稳定性条件下当 DS-SNR≥1 可精确收敛到真实子空间，从而给出尖锐相变结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有复杂性理论表明 DS-SNR<1 时问题计算困难，而 DS-SNR>1 时在一般位置假设下可由实用算法求解；但临界边界 DS-SNR=1 处实用算法的表现尚不清楚。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者分析 Tyler's M-estimator（TME），在 majorization-minimization 框架下分解 TME 迭代过程，并引入比既有一般位置假设更弱的新稳定性条件来证明收敛性。

Result: 证明在新的稳定性条件下，TME 对 DS-SNR≥1 均能精确收敛到真实 d 维子空间，特别解决了临界点 DS-SNR=1 的行为问题。

Conclusion: 该工作为 RSR 中 TME 的成功条件给出更精确刻画，建立了与理论硬度阈值相匹配的尖锐相变，并放宽了先前分析所需的几何假设。

Abstract: Robust Subspace Recovery (RSR) aims to identify an underlying d-dimensional subspace from a dataset heavily corrupted by outliers. Complexity-theoretic results establish a threshold for the problem's computational hardness based on the dimension-scaled signal-to-noise ratio (DS-SNR): the problem is SSE-hard when the DS-SNR is strictly less than 1, and solvable via practical algorithms when it is greater than 1 under general position assumptions. However, the exact behavior of practical algorithms at the critical boundary DS-SNR = 1 has remained unknown. This work resolves the behavior of Tyler's M-estimator (TME) at this critical boundary, consequently establishing a sharp phase transition. Specifically, we prove that TME converges exactly to the true subspace for DS-SNR \geq 1 under a new stability condition, which is less restrictive than the general position assumptions used in prior literature. Our analysis utilizes a decomposition of the TME iterates within a majorization-minimization framework.

</details>


### [157] [Information Rate Decomposition for Noisy Nanopore Channels with Geometric Duplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06808)
*Brendon McBain,Emanuele Viterbo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究带记忆的噪声重复信道信息率，面向纳米孔 DNA 测序场景，提出信息率分解、软对齐分析与可计算下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米孔测序信号同时受到符号间干扰（多个碱基位于孔中）和随机采样重复（碱基产生随机数量样本）的影响，使完整信道的信息率难以直接理论分析。

Method: 作者将信息率分解为两个可解释项：辅助 ISI 信道的内在记忆项，以及随机重复导致的片段边界不确定性项；进一步用与 Soft-DTW 相关的软对齐泛函研究第二项，并建立强 AEP 与基于强信息稳定性的 Markov 约束编码定理证明。

Result: 提出了依赖相邻纳米孔电平跳跃距离分布的信息率下界，该下界可用于刻画信道同步性，并为 Oxford 纳米孔测序器的可达速率计算提供可处理框架。

Conclusion: 该工作通过结构化分解将复杂的带记忆重复信道分析转化为更可解释、可计算的组成部分，为纳米孔测序信道的信息论建模与速率估计提供了理论工具。

Abstract: This paper studies information rates of noisy duplication channels with memory, motivated by nanopore DNA sequencing. In nanopore sequencing, the measured signal is affected by both inter-symbol interference (ISI), caused by multiple DNA bases residing in the pore, and random sample duplications, where variable translocation speed causes each base to generate a random number of samples. These two effects make direct theoretical analysis difficult. To address this, we derive a new decomposition of the information rate into two interpretable terms: one associated with the intrinsic memory of an auxiliary ISI channel, and another that captures the uncertainty in the segment boundaries caused by random duplications. This decomposition separates the dominant channel distortions and replaces the direct analysis of the full channel with two more readily interpretable components. We then study the second term through a soft alignment functional closely related to Soft-DTW, which enables strong AEP results and an alternative proof of the Markov-constrained coding theorem based on strong information stability. Finally, we develop a lower bound on the information rate that depends on the distribution of jump distances between adjacent nanopore levels. This bound gives a simple geometric explanation of channel synchronisability and provides a tractable framework for computing achievable rates of Oxford nanopore sequencers.

</details>


### [158] [Weighted Sum-Rate Enhancement for Flexible Intelligent Metasurface-Assisted Multicell Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06845)
*Hanwen Hu,Jiancheng An,Lu Gan,Hongbin Li,Naofal Al-Dhahir,George K. Karagiannidis,Arumugam Nallanathan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究柔性智能超表面（FIM）辅助多小区多用户 MISO 系统中的加权和速率最大化问题，通过联合优化基站发射波束成形、反射相移矩阵与表面形状来提升通信性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统刚性可重构智能表面（RIS）只能调控电磁反射参数，而 FIM 还能沿表面法向调整各散射单元位移，从而改变整体表面形态。这一额外自由度有望在复杂多小区场景中增强波束成形能力并抑制干扰，因此需要建立相应的联合优化框架。

Method: 作者将问题建模为受发射功率、单位模反射系数和表面形变范围约束的非凸优化问题。为处理变量高度耦合，提出交替优化框架：利用 WMMSE 方法进行等价重构，并采用 BCD 迭代更新变量；相移矩阵用黎曼共轭梯度（RCG）优化，FIM 表面形状用投影梯度下降（PGD）优化，发射波束成形向量则以闭式形式求解。

Result: 摘要中未给出具体仿真数值或性能提升幅度，但表明所提算法能够有效求解联合波束成形、相移和表面形状优化问题，以提升多小区多用户系统的加权和速率。

Conclusion: FIM 的可形变能力为无线传播环境调控提供了新的自由度。通过 WMMSE-BCD 框架联合优化通信与几何参数，可在复杂多小区 MU-MISO 场景中实现更优的速率性能和干扰管理。

Abstract: Flexible intelligent metasurface (FIM) technology has emerged as a promising technology for enhancing wireless communication performance by dynamically reshaping the propagation environment. Compared with conventional rigid reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS), an FIM is composed of multiple electromagnetic (EM) scattering units, each of which can flexibly modify its displacement in the direction normal to the surface, thereby cooperatively morphing the overall surface shape. This additional degree of freedom (DoF) enables improved beamforming and interference mitigation, particularly in complex multicell scenarios. In this paper, an optimization problem for maximizing the weighted sum-rate (WSR) in a multicell multi-user multiple-input single-output (MU-MISO) system assisted by an FIM deployed at the cell boundary is investigated. We jointly optimize the transmit beamforming at the base station (BS), the phase shift matrix, and the FIM surface shape, subject to constraints on the transmit power budget, unit-modulus reflection coefficients, and surface shape morphing range. Due to the non-convex objective function with highly coupled variables, solving the formulated optimization problem is challenging. To tackle this challenge, we propose an efficient alternating optimization framework that leverages the weighted minimum mean square error (WMMSE) method to reformulate the problem and the block coordinate descent (BCD) algorithm to iteratively update the variables. Specifically, the Riemannian conjugate gradient (RCG) algorithm is leveraged to optimize the phase shift matrix, while the projected gradient descent (PGD) method is adopted to optimize the surface shape of the FIM. Additionally, the optimal beamforming vectors are obtained in closed form.

</details>


### [159] [Mutual Information Optimization via K-Recursion and Automatic Differentiation for Linear Gaussian Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06982)
*Tadashi Wadayama,Na Siqi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向线性高斯DAG的可微端到端互信息优化框架，通过K-recursion传播节点间协方差，并结合自动微分与投影梯度上升实现拓扑无关优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通信网络设计常依赖特定拓扑的闭式梯度或分散建模，难以统一处理MIMO预编码、AF中继、RIS辅助信道以及分支/合并拓扑；同时在全局约束（如总发射功率）下进行网络级互信息优化需要一种通用、可微、端到端的方法。

Method: 将网络建模为线性高斯有向无环图，使用K-recursion按拓扑序解析传播所有节点对协方差，特别包含非相邻节点的交叉协方差以正确处理分支与合并路径；由协方差构造闭式log-det互信息目标，并利用复值反向模式自动微分获得各可控因子的精确Wirtinger梯度，再通过投影梯度上升在全局约束下最大化互信息。

Result: 同一套拓扑无关实现被用于单链路MIMO、diamond DAG、两跳AF中继和输入协方差成形等场景；在存在经典水填充解的设置中达到水填充最优，在非单链路拓扑中无需专用梯度公式即可提升互信息；在一个11节点5层多层高斯网络上也展示了对复杂拓扑的适用性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种统一、拓扑无关且可自动微分的线性高斯DAG互信息优化方案，避免为不同网络拓扑手工推导梯度，并能在多类通信网络设计问题中有效工作。

Abstract: We present a differentiable framework for end-to-end mutual information (MI) optimization over linear Gaussian directed acyclic graphs (DAGs). The framework targets network-wide design under global constraints, such as a total transmit power budget, and covers MIMO precoding, amplify-and-forward relays, RIS-aided channels, and branching/merging topologies within a common linear Gaussian model. Its core ingredient is a \emph{K-recursion} that analytically propagates all node-pair covariances along the DAG in topological order, including non-adjacent cross-covariances that are necessary for correctly handling branching and merging paths. The resulting covariances yield a closed-form log-determinant expression for the end-to-end MI as a smooth function of the controllable factors. Complex-valued reverse-mode automatic differentiation on this K-recursion then returns the exact Wirtinger gradient at every controllable factor in a single backward sweep, and projected gradient ascent (PGA) is used to maximize the MI under the global constraints. Because no closed-form gradient expression per topology is required, the same topology-agnostic implementation applies to any linear Gaussian DAG. A single topology-agnostic implementation is applied to four representative DAG classes: single-link MIMO, a diamond DAG, a two-hop AF relay, and input-covariance shaping. The same implementation reaches the classical water-filling optimum in the settings where it is available and yields MI improvements in non-single-link topologies without using topology-specific gradient formulas. A further experiment on a multi-layer Gaussian network (11 nodes, 5 layers) illustrates applicability to nontrivial multi-layer topologies for which no closed-form gradient is available.

</details>


### [160] [Environment-Division Multiple Access: an Enabler for AI-Native Multiple Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07025)
*Zhiguo Ding*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出环境划分多址（EDMA）作为一种新型多址方式，强调利用与重构无线传播环境，并探讨其与AI原生通信网络的结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多址技术主要围绕时间、频率、码、空间等资源进行用户区分；随着智能超表面、柔性天线和AI原生网络的发展，传播环境本身可被感知、建模和主动调控，因此需要一种以环境特征为核心的新型多址范式。

Method: 文章概念性地定义EDMA，概述其关键性质：利用无线传播环境特征、结合先进柔性天线、主动重构传播环境；同时区分两类AI与多址结合方式，即AI辅助EDMA和AI原生EDMA，并讨论相关开放问题。

Result: 文章给出了EDMA的基本框架和应用图景，说明AI工具可用于环境感知、信道建模、资源分配、波束/天线配置和环境重构决策，并进一步指出AI原生设计可使多址机制与网络智能深度融合。

Conclusion: EDMA有望成为面向未来AI原生通信网络的重要多址方向，但其理论建模、性能边界、实时智能控制、硬件协同、协议设计和可扩展部署仍需深入研究。

Abstract: In this article, a new type of multiple access, termed Environment-Division Multiple Access (EDMA), is introduced and its interaction with AI-native communication networks is illustrated. In particular, the key properties of EDMA, such as utilizing the features of wireless propagation environments, integrating advanced flexible antennas, and proactively reconfiguring propagation environments, are described. The article also illustrates two types of applications of AI tools to multiple access, namely AI-assisted EDMA and AI-native EDMA. Finally, open problems and important directions for future research in AI-assisted EDMA are discussed.

</details>


### [161] [Information-Theoretic Bounds for Sparse Covariance Estimation in the Vertical-Split Distributed Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07124)
*Jing Yee Tan,Guangyue Han*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究垂直切分（按特征切分）分布式协方差矩阵估计中，交叉协方差矩阵具备逐元素 s-稀疏性时的极小极大误差、通信复杂度与样本复杂度，并证明稀疏性可显著降低代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作主要给出了稠密、无结构交叉协方差矩阵在垂直切分场景下的近紧界。作者希望回答：若交叉协方差 C_{21} 是逐元素 s-稀疏的，是否能降低通信预算和样本需求；这与水平切分均值估计中“稀疏性不降低通信成本”的结论形成对比。

Method: 论文使用 Fano 方法建立极小极大下界；构造显式稀疏 packing，基于 Varshamov–Gilbert 型论证生成带符号的部分置换矩阵；并结合条件强数据处理不等式推导通信受限下的信息下界。上界方面，提出基于覆盖网量化与逐项硬阈值化的可达方案。

Result: 得到每个参与方通信预算下界 B_k = Ω(σ^4 d_k s' log(d_1 d_2/s')/ε^2)，交叉协方差估计样本复杂度下界 m = Ω(σ^4 s' log(d_1 d_2/s')/ε^2)，其中 s'=s∧d_min。对于 1-稀疏情形，相比稠密情形从 d_1d_2 降至 log(d_1d_2)，呈指数级改进。提出的算法在多对数因子内匹配下界。

Conclusion: 在垂直特征切分的分布式交叉协方差估计中，元素级稀疏性确实能降低通信与样本复杂度；论文给出了近紧的理论刻画，并表明其结论区别于水平切分均值估计中的负面结果。

Abstract: We study the minimax estimation error for distributed covariance matrix estimation in the vertical-split (feature-split) setting, where two agents each observe different coordinates of $m$ i.i.d. sub-Gaussian samples and communicate a limited number of bits to a central server. While Rahmani et al. [2025] established nearly tight bounds for dense (unstructured) cross-covariance matrices, we investigate whether imposing elementwise $s$-sparsity on the cross-covariance $C_{21}$ can reduce the required communication and sample complexity. In contrast to the horizontal-split setting, where Braverman et al. [2016] showed that sparsity does not reduce communication cost for mean estimation, we prove that sparsity does help for cross-covariance estimation in the vertical split.
  Specifically, we establish minimax lower bounds showing that the communication budget per agent scales as $B_k = Ω(σ^4 d_k\, s' \log(d_1 d_2/s')/\varepsilon^2)$ and the sample complexity for cross-covariance estimation as $m = Ω(σ^4\, s' \log(d_1 d_2/s')/\varepsilon^2)$, where $s' = s \wedge d_{\min}$. For the $1$-sparse case, this yields an exponential improvement from $d_1 d_2$ to $\log(d_1 d_2)$ compared to the dense rate. Our lower bounds are established via Fano's method with an explicit sparse packing using a Varshamov--Gilbert-type argument for signed partial permutation matrices combined with the Conditional Strong Data Processing Inequality of Rahmani et al. [2025]. We show the bounds are tight with a matching achievable scheme, based on covering-net quantization and entry-wise hard thresholding, that attains the $s$-sparse lower bound up to polylogarithmic factors.

</details>


### [162] [The Synthesis-Sequencing Channel for DNA-based Data Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07216)
*Keshav Goyal,Samuel Pearson,João Ribeiro,Serge Kas Hanna*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了用于 DNA 数据存储的“合成-测序信道”两阶段模型，并在二元对称错误假设下给出了信息论容量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 DNA 存储信道模型通常将合成与测序过程简化处理，难以同时区分合成后的物理覆盖度与读出后的测序覆盖度，也常假设不同 reads 的错误相互独立，因而对真实 DNA 存储流程中的覆盖偏置与误差耦合刻画不足。

Method: 作者构建包含合成阶段与测序阶段的两阶段信道模型，将合成错误和测序错误分别建模为可能具有不同翻转概率的二元对称信道。在温和参数条件下，通过推导相匹配的 converse 上界与 achievability 下界，建立该信道的信息论容量。

Result: 结果表明，该模型自然产生覆盖偏置，并揭示物理覆盖度、合成错误率、测序覆盖度和测序错误率之间存在多重权衡，这些因素共同决定可靠 DNA 数据存储的最大可达速率。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了更贴近实际 DNA 存储流程的信道建模框架，并从信息论角度明确了合成与测序两阶段误差及覆盖度对存储容量的影响。

Abstract: We introduce and study the synthesis-sequencing channel, a two-stage model for DNA-based data storage that jointly captures synthesis and sequencing effects. The synthesis-sequencing channel provides a more nuanced and realistic model of the DNA storage process compared to prior work, as it distinguishes between physical coverage after synthesis and sequencing coverage after readout, relaxes the assumption of independent errors across reads, and naturally induces coverage bias through the composition of synthesis and sequencing stages. We establish the information-theoretic capacity of this channel by deriving matching converse and achievability bounds for the case where synthesis and sequencing errors are modeled by binary symmetric channels with possibly different error probabilities, under mild assumptions on the channel parameters. Our results reveal multiple trade-offs between physical coverage, synthesis errors, sequencing coverage, and sequencing errors that influence the maximum achievable rate for reliable data storage.

</details>


### [163] [The Capacity of Information-Theoretic Secure Aggregation in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07277)
*Lanxin Yi,Jinbao Zhu,Kai Wan,Xiaohu Tang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究无可信第三方、一般用户间密钥分发机制下的 T-合谋信息论安全聚合，完整刻画随机性、密钥分发通信与聚合通信三者的容量区域，并给出显式最优构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全聚合理论通常假设相关随机密钥由可信第三方提供，或通过预设群组结构生成，且常忽略建立相关密钥所需通信成本，因此在一般密钥分发机制下的基础极限尚不清楚。

Method: 提出包含密钥分发阶段和更新聚合阶段的两阶段框架，允许用户间通信生成任意密钥分发机制；构造新的信息论安全聚合方案，并给出匹配的 converse 证明以刻画容量区域；进一步设计任意大小至少为 N 的有限域上的显式确定性容量达成构造。

Result: 完整刻画了安全随机性、密钥分发通信和聚合通信之间的容量区域；证明最优性能仅需成对共享密钥即可实现，并可通过 Diffie–Hellman 密钥交换落地；相较 Google 经典安全聚合方案，在保持相同聚合通信开销的同时减少随机掩码密钥数量。

Conclusion: 该工作消除了对可信第三方和预设密钥结构的依赖，从信息论角度给出一般密钥分发安全聚合的最优资源权衡，并提供具有实际实现潜力的显式最优方案。

Abstract: Secure aggregation allows a server to aggregate users' local updates while preserving update privacy. Existing information-theoretic problems typically assume that correlated random keys are provided by a trusted third party (TTP) or generated via prescribed groupwise structures, while the communication cost for establishing such correlated keys is often ignored. Consequently, the fundamental limits under general key-distribution mechanisms remain unknown. In this paper, we study the $T$-colluding information-theoretic secure aggregation problem with $N$ users under a general two-phase framework consisting of a key distribution phase and an update aggregation phase. Unlike prior work, we model key distribution through user-to-user communication and allow arbitrary user-generated key-distribution mechanisms, eliminating TTP or prescribed structures. This enables a joint characterization of three resources: randomness for security, key-distribution communication, and aggregation communication. We completely characterize the capacity region among these three resources by constructing a novel secure aggregation scheme together with a matching information-theoretic converse. In particular, we develop an explicit deterministic capacity-achieving construction over any finite field of size at least $N$, whereas most existing schemes either rely on TTP or employ randomized or existential constructions over sufficiently large finite fields. We further show that the optimal performance can be achieved using only pairwise shared keys, enabling implementation via Diffie--Hellman key exchange. Compared with Google's seminal secure aggregation scheme, the proposed scheme requires fewer random masking keys while preserving the same aggregation communication overhead.

</details>


### [164] [Letting Homogeneity Entropy Select S-Pairs in Buchberger's Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07321)
*Uzma Shafiq,Matthew England,AmirHosein Sadeghimanesh,Nayyar Zaidi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于 Buchberger 算法的 S-pair 选择策略 Homogeneity Entropy，并在不同数据集上验证其适用性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Groebner 基计算中，S-polynomial 的构造顺序会显著影响 Buchberger 算法效率；传统 Degree、Normal、Sugar 启发式及机器学习方法并不能在所有多项式系统分布上稳定最优，因此需要探索新的选择准则。

Method: 作者提出 Homogeneity Entropy 策略，利用 S-polynomial 单项式次数分布的信息论度量来指导 S-pair 选择；实现该策略，并在随机生成多项式系统与 PHCpack 现实问题基准数据集上与经典策略进行实验比较。

Result: 在随机多项式数据集上，Homogeneity Entropy 显著优于经典策略；但在 PHCpack 数据集上，经典策略表现更好。进一步实验表明策略性能与数据形态密切相关。

Conclusion: Homogeneity Entropy 是符号计算算法优化中一种新的信息论导向方法，在特定数据分布下具有实际性能收益，但其适用性依赖问题结构，未来可结合数据特征进行自适应策略选择。

Abstract: We present a novel S-pair selection strategy called Homogeneity Entropy, for deciding the sequence of S-polynomials to construct in Buchberger's algorithm to compute a Groebner basis. The strategy uses an information theoretic measure derived from the distribution of degrees among the monomials of the S-polynomial: a very different approach to the classical heuristics such as Degree, Normal and Sugar, or indeed the more recent machine learning approaches to the problem. We implement this strategy and evaluate it on two different datasets: (1) variations of randomly generated polynomial systems with controlled numbers of variables, degrees, and densities; and (2) the PHCpack benchmark dataset sourced from real world problems. The Homogeneity Entropy strategy significantly outperforms classical strategies on random polynomial datasets, but on the PHCpack dataset the classical strategies perform better. This suggests the right strategy varies with the shape of the data and we explore this in several experiments. The new strategy offers practically meaningful gains on certain distributions, and represents the first use of such information-theoretic guidance in the optimisation of symbolic computation algorithms.

</details>


### [165] [Rate Loss in Quantum Channels with Classical State and Applications for Quantum Broadcast Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07409)
*Igor Bernard,Arun Padakandla*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究经典-量子信道中接收端缺乏信道状态信息导致的可达速率严格损失，并展示结构化陪集码在特定多用户量子广播信道中可优于非结构化 IID 随机码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 CQ 信道中，接收端无法获得信道状态信息可能会降低通信速率；作者希望刻画这种 rate loss 的存在条件，并探索是否有编码策略能突破传统 IID 随机码的性能限制。

Method: 首先识别一类非交换的 CQ 信道，并通过解析证明说明其存在严格速率损失；随后将该结论扩展到非交换三用户 CQ 广播信道，比较陪集码结构化策略与常规非结构化 IID 编码策略的可达性能。

Result: 证明了某些非交换 CQ 信道在接收端缺乏信道状态信息时存在严格速率损失；同时证明在非交换 3 用户 CQ 广播信道中，基于陪集码的策略能够严格优于传统 IID 随机码策略。

Conclusion: 非交换性是导致 CQ 信道速率损失的重要机制之一；结构化编码（如陪集码）在多用户 CQ 场景中具有超越传统 IID 随机编码的潜力。

Abstract: We consider the problem of \textit{rate loss} - a strict penalty suffered in achievable rates due to the lack of channel state information at the receiver (Rx) of a classical-quantum (CQ) channel. First, we identify non-commutative CQ channels and analytically prove a rate loss. Building on this, we next prove that coset-code-based strategies can strictly outperform conventional unstructured IID-code-based strategies for non-commutative 3-user CQ broadcast channels.

</details>


### [166] [Sort, Partition, Randomize: Optimal Binary Hypothesis Testing under Local Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07443)
*Elena Ghazi,Jawad Nasser,Flavio Calmon,Ibrahim Issa*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究二元假设检验中 ε-本地差分隐私机制的最优设计，提出排序-分割-随机化（SPR）结构，并给出多项式时间精确动态规划算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究虽揭示了最优本地差分隐私机制的一些结构性质，但求解算法通常为指数时间，难以在有限字母表和完整隐私参数范围内高效计算精确最优机制。

Method: 将观测字母按似然比排序，证明对任意 ε 和任意 f-散度效用目标，都存在一个最优机制：把排序后的字母表划分为若干连续块，并对块标签执行随机响应；基于该 SPR 表征构造精确动态规划。

Result: 得到 O(k^3) 时间的精确最优机制计算算法；若输出数预算为 ℓ，则复杂度可推广为 O(ℓ k^2)。该结果适用于总变差、KL、hockey-stick 等 f-散度目标。

Conclusion: SPR 结构给出了二元假设检验下 ε-本地差分隐私最优机制的清晰刻画，使得在非渐近、全隐私范围内高效计算和分析精确最优机制成为可能。

Abstract: We study optimal design of $\varepsilon$-locally differentially private mechanisms for binary hypothesis testing. Each observation is drawn from one of two known distributions $P_0,P_1$ on a finite alphabet of size $k$, privatized by a mechanism $Q$, and then used to infer which distribution generated the data. We measure testing utility using an $f$-divergence, including total variation, KL, and hockey-stick divergences, between the two induced output distributions. Previous work established structural properties of optimal mechanisms, but only yielded exponential-time algorithms. We prove a sharp structure: for every $\varepsilon$ and every $f$-divergence objective, after sorting the alphabet by likelihood ratio, there exists an optimal mechanism that partitions the sorted alphabet into contiguous blocks and applies randomized response to the block label. We call this class Sort-Partition-Randomize (SPR). This characterization yields an exact dynamic program that computes an optimal mechanism in $O(k^3)$ time, and more generally in $O(\ell k^2)$ time with an $\ell$-output budget. Our results make it possible to efficiently compute and characterize the exact optimum across the full privacy range, beyond asymptotic privacy regimes.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Detecting and Mitigating Bias by Treating Fairness as a Symmetry Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06514)
*Nishit Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将机器学习公平性中的偏差形式化为“对称性破缺”，并通过损失正则化恢复对敏感属性反事实翻转的不变性；在合成数据上显著降低公平性违规，代价是小幅准确率下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险社会经济场景中的机器学习模型常出现偏差。作者希望提出一种不依赖因果图、计算成本低、可推广到二元敏感属性的公平性约束框架，以处理传统基准中未充分覆盖的局部歧视来源。

Method: 将公平性定义为：在保持能力/绩效相关特征不变时，对敏感属性进行反事实 bit-flip 后，分类器输出应保持不变。基于这一对称性观点，作者在训练目标中加入损失正则项，用作“恢复对称性”的机制，并在具有不同噪声、相关性和偏差水平的四个合成数据集上评估。

Result: 实验显示，该框架可实现超过 90% 的公平性违规减少，同时准确率损失约为 5%。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种轻量、无需因果图知识的公平性正则化方案，适用于可表示为二元翻转的敏感属性，并可能用于传统公平性基准之外的应用场景。

Abstract: Machine learning systems deployed in high stakes socioeconomic settings routinely display bias. We formalize bias as a symmetry breaking operation: a classifier is fair if its outputs remain invariant under the counterfactual operation of switching a sensitive attribute, with merit features held fixed. We implement loss based regularization as a symmetry restoring mechanism and evaluate the framework on four synthetic datasets with varying levels of noise, correlation, and bias. The framework achieves upwards of 90\% violation reduction, with accuracy costs around 5\%. This framework does not require causal graph knowledge, is computationally lightweight, and generalizes to any sensitive attribute definable as a bit-flip, making it suitable for contexts where local sources of discrimination remain absent from mainstream benchmarks.

</details>


### [168] [DiBS: Diffusion-Informed Branch Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06518)
*Bo Liu,Yuan Xie,Yuan Gao,Xiaolong Luo,Peng Ye,Tao Chen,Fujun Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 DiBS：用扩散模型指导数独求解中的分支取值排序，同时保留符号求解器的完备性；在 Royle 17-clue 基准上显著降低搜索节点、回溯和长尾搜索成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数独求解方法主要分为启发式符号求解与深度学习求解。学习式方法通常缺乏硬正确性保证，而完备符号求解器在困难实例上容易遭遇长尾搜索。论文旨在结合两者优势：既保持求解完备性，又减少错误分支顺序带来的高昂搜索代价。

Method: 作者提出 DiBS，将扩散模型作为分支选择/排序指南嵌入符号搜索过程。符号求解器负责保证完整性与正确性，扩散模型根据当前部分赋值和轻量级一致性信号，对候选取值进行排序，从而引导搜索优先探索更有希望的分支。论文还给出理论分析，解释该引导机制的有效性。

Result: 在具有挑战性的 Royle 17-clue Sudoku benchmark 上，DiBS 相比强启发式基线显著减少搜索成本，尤其体现在搜索节点数、回溯次数以及长尾分位数表现上。实验表明，在困难实例中，学习到的全局结构引导能有效降低分支排序错误的代价。

Conclusion: DiBS 展示了神经生成模型与完备符号搜索结合的有效性：扩散模型不直接替代求解器，而是提供全局分支排序信号，从而在不牺牲正确性保证的前提下提升困难约束满足问题的搜索效率。

Abstract: Sudoku is a representative constraint satisfaction problem that requires global structural reasoning under strict discrete constraints. The existing works of solving Sudoku mainly focus on two dominant approaches, i.e., traditional heuristic and deep learning solver. However, they suffer from two complementary limitations: learning-based solvers lack hard correctness guarantees, while complete symbolic solvers are still prone to long-tail search. To address these shortcomings, we propose a novel diffusion model-guided approach, termed as DiBS, for the branch selection search process. Specifically, DiBS keeps the symbolic solver complete and uses the diffusion model as a branch-ordering guide. The core method is ranking candidate values under the current partial assignment and lightweight consistency signal. Furthermore, we provide an in-depth theoretical proof to reveal how it works and why it works. Experiments on the challenging Royle 17-clue Sudoku benchmark show that our DiBS substantially reduces search cost relative to strong heuristic baselines, especially in nodes, backtracks, and long-tail percentiles. Besides, these results confirm that learned global guidance is effective on hard instances where branch-order mistakes are most expensive. All codes are available at https://github.com/shanxierdan/DiBS.

</details>


### [169] [SafeGene: Reusable Adapters for Transferable Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06519)
*Yanghan Wang,Zhiqiang Kou,Fu Feng,Jing Wang,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 SafeGene：一种可复用的安全适配器模块，用于在同一架构兼容模型族内跨任务恢复微调后受损的安全对齐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重大语言模型在下游微调后可能削弱原有安全对齐，使模型更容易受到恶意提示影响；由于模型会随着新任务数据或用户交互不断更新，安全恢复成为反复出现的问题。

Method: SafeGene 将安全能力视为与任务更新解耦的独立适配器表示：先从已对齐模型与退化模型之间的差异中提取安全相关表征，再通过数据感知的层选择形成可迁移安全向量，最后在下游任务适配模型中用少样本逐层系数重校准来注入安全能力。

Result: 在多个模型家族、下游任务和安全评测器上的实验表明，SafeGene 能降低有害响应率，同时保持下游任务性能；在安全性与实用性的权衡上优于代表性安全适配方法。

Conclusion: SafeGene 将安全恢复从单个模型的事后修补转化为可复用、跨任务迁移的安全能力表达，为频繁微调的开放权重模型提供了更高效的安全维护方案。

Abstract: Open-weight LLMs are increasingly fine-tuned into customized assistants, but downstream fine-tuning can weaken safety alignment and make models more vulnerable to malicious prompts, even when the training data is not intentionally harmful. This creates a recurring safety recovery problem as target models are repeatedly updated with new task data or user interactions. We propose SafeGene, a reusable safety-adapter module designed for cross-task reuse within each architecture-compatible model family. Rather than treating safety recovery as a model-specific repair step, SafeGene treats safety capability as an independent, reusable adapter representation decoupled from task-specific updates. This representation is obtained from aligned--degraded model discrepancies, refined into task-transferable safety vectors through data-aware layer selection, and expressed in each downstream task-adapted model via few-shot layer-wise coefficient recalibration. Experiments across multiple model families, downstream tasks, and safety judges show that SafeGene-enhanced models reduce harmful response rates while maintaining downstream performance, outperforming representative safe adaptation methods in safety--utility trade-off.

</details>


### [170] [Lean4Agent: Formal Modeling and Verification for Agent Workflow and Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06523)
*Ruida Wang,Jerry Huang,Pengcheng Wang,Xuanqing Liu,Luyang Kong,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Lean4Agent：用 Lean4 依赖类型形式语言对 LLM Agent 的多步工作流进行建模、验证、调试，并通过 LeanEvolve 自动改进工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM Agent 虽具备一定自主执行能力，但多步工作流缺少形式化的规格说明、验证与调试机制；自然语言描述存在歧义，导致语义一致性和执行失败定位困难。作者借鉴数学中用形式语言消除自然语言歧义的思想，引入 Lean4 来规范 Agent 行为。

Method: 构建 Lean4Agent 框架，包含 FormalAgentLib：一个可扩展的 Lean4 库，用于在显式假设下形式化建模并验证 Agent 工作流的语义一致性，同时利用执行轨迹定位运行时失败；进一步提出 LeanEvolve，基于 FormalAgentLib 的验证与诊断结果修订工作流以提升能力。

Result: 在 SWE-Bench-Verified 的困难子集和 ELAIP-Bench 子集上，覆盖 5 个领先 LLM 的实验显示，通过验证的工作流相比未通过验证的工作流平均性能提升 11.94%；LeanEvolve 在 SWE 任务上进一步平均提升 7.47%。

Conclusion: Lean4Agent 证明了使用表达力强的依赖类型形式语言对 LLM Agent 行为进行建模、验证和改进的可行性，为形式化验证 Agent 工作流开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Equipping Large Language Models (LLMs) to execute reliable multi-step workflows has become a central challenge in artificial intelligence. Despite recent advances in LLMs' agentic capabilities, most agent systems still lack formal methods for specifying, verifying, and debugging their workflow and execution trajectories. This challenge mirrors a long-standing problem in mathematics, where the ambiguity of natural languages (NLs) motivates the development of formal languages (FLs). Inspired by this paradigm, we propose **Lean4Agent**, to the best of our knowledge, the first framework that uses Lean4, a dependent-type FL to model and verify agent behavior. **Lean4Agent** launches **FormalAgentLib**, an extensible Lean4 library for formally modeling and verifying agent workflows' semantic consistency under explicit assumptions, and enabling localization of execution-time failures revealed by trajectories. Building on **FormalAgentLib**, we further develop **LeanEvolve**, which applies results in **FormalAgentLib** to revise workflows to enhance its capability. Extensive experiments on a hard problem subset of SWE-Bench-Verified and a subset of ELAIP-Bench across 5 leading LLMs indicate that the verification-passing workflows outperform the failing ones by an average of **11.94%**, and **LeanEvolve** further improves SWE performance by **7.47%** on average. Furthermore, **Lean4Agent** establishes a foundation for a new field of using expressive dependent-type FL to formally model and verify agent behavior.

</details>


### [171] [CrowdMath: A Dataset of Crowdsourced Mathematical Research Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06526)
*Sherin Muckatira,Jesse Geneson,Slava Gerovitch,Pavel Etingof,Mikhail Gronas,Anna Rumshisky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 CrowdMath 数据集，用于评估大语言模型理解协作式开放数学问题求解过程的能力；实验显示模型能较好预测讨论的局部走向，但难以识别单个贡献在证明推进中的功能角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学推理基准多关注定义明确、答案完整的问题，无法刻画真实协作研究中常见的部分进展、错误发现、修正推理和逐步综合证明等过程，因此需要新的评测设置。

Method: 作者构建了来自 MIT PRIMES--AoPS CrowdMath 项目的 164 条专家标注进展链，每条链从开放问题陈述延伸到完整证明，并对帖子进行功能角色标注；随后定义下一帖预测和帖子角色分类等任务，评测六个前沿模型。

Result: 模型在下一帖预测上达到 83-88% 准确率，说明其能跟随数学讨论的局部流程；但在帖子角色分类上表现较弱，最佳模型宏平均 F1 仅为 0.42。

Conclusion: CrowdMath 揭示了当前大语言模型在“解答规范数学题”和“理解协作式数学进展”之间的能力差距，尤其是在判断贡献的功能意义方面仍存在明显不足。

Abstract: Large language models have made substantial progress on mathematical reasoning, but existing benchmarks typically evaluate well-specified problems with final answers, step-by-step solutions, or complete proofs. They do not capture collaborative open-problem solving: a setting in which participants propose partial arguments, identify gaps or errors in prior steps, repair flawed reasoning, and gradually synthesize incremental contributions into a proof. We introduce CrowdMath, a dataset of 164 expert-annotated progress chains from the MIT PRIMES--Art of Problem Solving (AoPS) CrowdMath program (2016-2025), a collaborative research initiative whose discussions have led to peer-reviewed publications. Each chain traces a multi-participant forum discussion from an open-problem statement to a completed proof. Posts are labeled by their functional roles in the evolving solution process, including partial progress, proof completion, erroneous reasoning, and error identification. We define evaluation tasks and benchmark six frontier models. Models achieve 83-88% accuracy on next-post prediction, suggesting that they can follow the local flow of mathematical discussion. However, they struggle to identify the functional significance of individual contributions with the best model achieving only 0.42 macro-F1 on post-role classification. CrowdMath exposes a gap between solving well-specified mathematical problems and understanding collaborative mathematical progress as it unfolds.

</details>


### [172] [Attack Selection in Agentic AI Control Evaluations Meaningfully Decreases Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06529)
*Catherine Ge-Wang,Tyler Crosse,Benjamin Hadad,Joachim Schaeffer,Ram Potham,Tyler Tracy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出，在AI控制评估中，具备“选择何时攻击/何时中止攻击”能力的选择性攻击者会显著降低系统的实测安全性，使现有评估可能过于乐观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI控制评估通常假设攻击者不具备策略性选择攻击时机的能力；但现实中，攻击者若能只在有利情境下发起攻击、在风险较高时中止攻击，会更难被监控器或有限人工审计发现。因此需要研究“攻击选择”对安全评估的影响。

Method: 作者将攻击选择分解为两个策略：start policy用于决定何时开始攻击，stop policy用于决定何时中止正在进行的攻击。研究在两个agentic环境BashArena和LinuxArena中，将红队攻击策略与蓝队监控机制进行对抗式控制评估，并比较引入start/stop策略前后的经验安全性变化。

Result: 在1%人工审计预算下，start policy在BashArena和LinuxArena上均使安全性降低约20个百分点；stop policy在BashArena上降低约20个百分点，在LinuxArena上降低约28个百分点。这些变化没有改变底层攻击能力，仅改变攻击时机选择。

Conclusion: 攻击选择能力会显著影响AI控制评估结果，现有评估可能高估系统对选择性攻击者的安全性。作者建议未来的控制评估、系统卡和安全论证应显式引出并测试攻击选择能力，以获得更现实的安全估计。

Abstract: An attacker that strategically chooses when to attack is much harder to catch than one that attacks indiscriminately. AI control is a safety framework for deploying capable but untrusted AI agents under the oversight of a weaker, trusted monitor and a limited human audit budget. Control evaluations stress-test these protocols by pitting a red-team attack policy against the blue-team monitor, but current evaluations typically assume attackers that do not strategically select when to attack. We study this capability, attack selection, in agentic settings by decomposing attack decisions into a start policy, which decides when an attacker should attack, and a stop policy, which decides when an attacker should abort an ongoing attack. Across two agentic settings, BashArena and LinuxArena, both policies substantially lower measured empirical safety without changing the underlying attack capability. At a 1% audit budget, our start policy reduces safety by 20pp on both BashArena and LinuxArena, and our stop policy reduces safety by 20pp on BashArena and 28pp on LinuxArena. These reductions should be interpreted as upper bounds on the effect of attack selection. Existing control evaluations may therefore yield overly optimistic safety estimates against selective attackers. We recommend that future evaluations, system cards, and safety cases elicit attack selection to produce more realistic safety estimates.

</details>


### [173] [CARVE-Q: Quantum-Proposed, Classically Certified Interactive Driving Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06531)
*Yifan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARVE提出一种用于自动驾驶被否决机动的可认证修复框架，并用CARVE-Q在保持经典安全验证权威的前提下，用量子最小值搜索加速多主体修复格的黑盒搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测式或博弈式规划能生成协作建议，但难以证明修复方案同时满足硬性交通规则、路权、责任边界、成本分摊与自车回退要求；多主体修复还面临组合爆炸。

Method: 构建有限修复格并输出结构化证书，记录约束规则、联合修复、按路权缩放的协作包络、责任加权成本分配和自车 fallback；进一步提出CARVE-Q，将量子最小值查找仅用于黑盒候选格搜索，所有安全判定由经典验证器屏蔽与认证。

Result: 理论上证明证书健全性、优先权不诱导、黑盒查询分离和有限精度可逆 oracle 可构造性；在保守 verifier-oracle 模型中，将最坏情形经典Θ(M)查询降为Durr-Hoyer/Grover的O(√M)高概率查询；实验在最多65,536个赋值的CARVE oracle上演示状态向量最小值查找，并在Lanelet2-grounded INTERACTION回放中实现100%路权遵守、100%责任一致和零优先权误报。

Conclusion: 该工作给出一种“量子提出、经典认证”的可信边界模式，使自动驾驶互动修复在可审计、责任有界的前提下获得潜在搜索加速。

Abstract: The critical question after a correct driving veto is not only whether a maneuver is unsafe, but whether the blocked interaction admits a lawful, auditable, and responsibility-bounded repair. Prediction and game-theoretic planners can suggest plausible cooperation, yet they do not return a proof that the repair respects hard rules, right-of-way, cost allocation, and ego fallback. We introduce CARVE, Certified Affordable Repair of Vetoed maneuvers via Envelopes, a certificate architecture for prediction-free interactive repair. Given a vetoed maneuver, CARVE constructs a finite repair lattice and emits a structured certificate recording the binding rule, selected joint repair, right-of-way-scaled cooperation envelope, responsibility-weighted cost split, and ego-only fallback. This certificate view reveals the algorithmic bottleneck: multi-owner repair induces a product lattice $M = \prod_j |\mathcal{A}_j|$. We therefore introduce CARVE-Q, a verifier-shielded quantum-AI search layer that applies quantum minimum finding only to this black-box lattice while leaving all safety authority classical. In the conservative verifier-oracle model, exact classical minimum finding requires $Θ(M)$ queries in the worst case, whereas Durr-Hoyer/Grover minimum finding uses $O(\sqrt{M})$ oracle queries with high probability. We prove verifier-shielded certificate soundness, priority non-elicitation, black-box query separation, and finite-precision reversible-oracle constructibility. We then demonstrate state-vector minimum finding on CARVE repair oracles up to 65,536 assignments and validate certificate preservation on Lanelet2-grounded INTERACTION replay with 100% right-of-way respect, 100% blame consistency, and zero priority false positives. The result is a trust-bounded quantum-AI pattern for certified autonomy: quantum proposes; CARVE certifies.

</details>


### [174] [Position: Don't Just "Fix it in Post": A Science of AI Must Study Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06533)
*Stella Biderman,Mohammad Aflah Khan,Niloofar Mireshghallah,Catherine Arnett,Fazl Barez,Naomi Saphra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张建立“AI科学”，核心是研究训练动态如何产生模型行为，而不只是训练后分析静态模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI研究常将模型视为训练完成后的固定产物，主要进行事后行为分析与修补；但模型行为源于数据、目标函数、架构与优化过程的共同演化，因此需要解释其形成机制。

Method: 作为立场论文，作者从科学史与科学哲学出发提出理论应满足的理解层级：基于早期训练信号预测结果、在轨迹异常时进行干预、设计更可靠地产生目标性质的训练流程；并综述机制可解释性、公平性、记忆化与简单性偏置等方向的进展。

Result: 论文指出，缩放定律已能较常规地预测损失，但将类似预测能力扩展到模型能力、偏差、鲁棒性和安全相关行为仍是关键挑战；同时识别了一系列具体开放问题。

Conclusion: 真正的AI科学应以训练动态为中心，发展可预测、可干预、可设计的理论框架，从而更可靠地理解并塑造模型行为。

Abstract: What would it mean to have a scientific understanding of AI? Models are not static objects: they are snapshots of time-evolving processes shaped by data, objectives, architectures, and optimization dynamics. Yet much of AI research treats models as fixed artifacts, analyzing behaviors after training rather than asking why they emerge. This position paper argues that a science of AI must move beyond post-hoc fixes and study the training dynamics that produce model behavior. Such a science should support progressively stronger forms of understanding: predicting outcomes from early training signals, intervening when trajectories go wrong, and ultimately designing training procedures that more reliably produce desired properties. Scaling laws have made prediction routine for loss; the challenge is extending this success to capabilities, biases, robustness, and safety-relevant behaviors. We articulate requirements for such theories grounded in the history and philosophy of science, examine progress in mechanistic interpretability, fairness, memorization, and simplicity bias, and identify concrete open problems.

</details>


### [175] [Accelerated Fourier SAT (AFSAT): Fully Realising a GPU-based Symmetric Pseudo-Boolean SAT Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06641)
*Cody J Christopher,Charles Gretton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AFSAT 是一个基于连续局部搜索的 GPU 加速伪布尔可满足性求解器，将 FastFourierSAT 原型工程化为可处理混合对称约束的实用系统，并通过 JAX 实现高并行、高稳定性和高吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 FastFourierSAT 证明了用傅里叶表示与连续局部搜索求解伪布尔可满足性的可行性，但仍停留在原型阶段，存在数值稳定性、运行效率、内存效率、异构约束支持和多加速器扩展方面的不足。

Method: 作者提出 AFSAT，利用 JAX 的纯函数组合、自动向量化、自动微分和 JIT 编译，在候选赋值批次上进行大规模并行连续局部搜索；支持单个实例中不同类型和长度的对称约束混合；通过紧凑表示、自动并行化、JAX 数组分片，以及定制离散傅里叶变换来缓解内存延迟和浮点表示问题。

Result: 相比原型 FastFourierSAT，AFSAT 在数值稳定性、运行时间性能和内存效率上有显著提升；在多加速器扩展时，通过 JAX array sharding 实现了近线性的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: AFSAT 将傅里叶方法与现代 GPU/JAX 编译栈结合，证明了基于连续局部搜索的伪布尔可满足性求解可以被工程化为高效、稳定、可扩展的求解器。

Abstract: We present Accelerated Fourier SAT (AFSAT), a GPU-accelerated solver for pseudo-Boolean satisfiability based on continuous local search (CLS). AFSAT realises the proof-of-concept approach, FastFourierSAT, into a fully-engineered solver supporting any heterogeneous mixture of symmetric constraint types and lengths within a single problem instance. Using the JAX compiler, AFSAT leverages pure function composition, automatic vectorisation, automatic differentiation, and just-in-time (JIT) compilation to perform massively parallel CLS across batches of candidate assignments. We demonstrate substantially improved numerical stability, runtime performance, and memory efficiency over the proof-of-concept. We achieve this by way of identifying and addressing various limitations that arise from memory latency and floating-point representation, as well as leveraging automatic parallelisation and compact representations. The inherent representational and stability limitations of floating point are partially addressed by a tailored discrete Fourier transform implementation. We achieve near-linear throughput when scaling to multiple accelerators via JAX array sharding.

</details>


### [176] [A Study of Parallel Continuous Local Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06656)
*Cody J Christopher,Charles Gretton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究将并行连续局部搜索（CLS）用于带对称伪布尔约束的布尔可满足性问题，通过连续松弛与经验实验评估其收敛行为和实际用途。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SAT求解在复杂约束和现代加速硬件场景下面临效率挑战；CLS将离散SAT问题转化为连续优化问题，可能更适合并行化和硬件加速，因此需要理解其实际表现、收敛特性及混合求解潜力。

Method: 将n变量伪布尔可满足性问题松弛为定义在n维超立方体上的可微目标函数；对可满足实例，目标函数全局极小值对应SAT满足赋值。作者通过经验实验研究冗余约束、部分赋值补全、局部搜索迭代步数与解质量分布等现象。

Result: 实验发现：（1）冗余约束未必加速收敛，反而可能抑制收敛；（2）CLS适合作为混合求解器中的子求解器，可快速补全部分赋值；（3）局部搜索会快速收敛到稳定的解质量分布，后续迭代收益递减，这与目标函数中鞍点密集的结构有关。

Conclusion: CLS在SAT求解中具有实用潜力，尤其适合现代加速硬件和混合求解框架；但其性能受约束表示和目标函数几何结构影响，盲目添加冗余约束可能适得其反。

Abstract: We study parallel Continuous Local Search (CLS) as a solution approach for Boolean satisfiability problems with symmetric pseudo-Boolean (PB) constraints. Here, the $n$-variable PB-satisfiability problem is relaxed to a continuous optimisation problem with a differentiable objective function on an $n$-dimensional hypercube. For satisfiable instances, the global minimisers of this optimisation problem correspond to satisfying assignments of the SAT problem at hand. We present several novel findings via empirical experiments: (i) redundant constraints can inhibit rather than accelerate convergence; (ii) CLS shows promise as a sub-solver in hybridised settings, quickly completing partial assignments; and (iii) local search rapidly converges to a stable distribution of solution quality (i.e., degree of satisfaction), due to saddle-dense objectives where additional solver steps yield diminishing returns. Our findings inform practical uses of CLS for SAT on modern accelerator hardware.

</details>


### [177] [AEGIS: A Backup Reflex for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06660)
*Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 AEGIS：用弱策略冻结激活上的轻量探针提前识别高风险操作步骤，并仅在必要步骤切换到强策略，从而提升长时程机器人操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作常因早期小错误导致状态逐步恶化，最终进入难以恢复的失败区域；这些失败征兆往往在真正失败前已可从策略内部表征中观察到，因此需要一种低成本、及时的风险检测与选择性干预机制。

Method: AEGIS 在弱策略的冻结激活上训练轻量级 activation probe，用于在轨迹早期或执行过程中预测高风险步骤；当探针触发警报时，系统将控制权临时切换给独立的更强策略，仅处理被判定为高风险的步骤。研究采用预注册分析方案、条件 recovered-task-rate 估计量、显式终止准则，并在 LIBERO-Spatial 上进行 common-random-number episode 对照实验。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Spatial 上，AEGIS 挽回了弱策略原本失败轨迹中的 10.1%，优于预算匹配的盲目升级 4.6% 和随机触发 placebo 5.1%。相对盲目升级提升 5.4 个百分点，p=8.5e-6；相对随机触发提升 5.0 个百分点，p=1.0e-4；bootstrap 置信区间排除零。AEGIS 仅在 38% 步骤启用强策略，早期窗口 AUROC 为 0.764，95% CI [0.70, 0.84]。

Conclusion: 结果表明，长时程机器人操作中的失败可通过弱策略内部激活提前预警；相比单纯增加强策略调用频率，关键收益来自更准确的干预时机。AEGIS 提供了一种计算效率较高、统计验证较充分的选择性策略升级框架。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation tends to fail gradually: one bad step degrades the state, and the policy spirals into a basin from which it cannot recover. The failure is often visible before it happens. We introduce AEGIS (Activation-probe Early-warning, Gated Inference Switching), a selective escalation method that uses a lightweight probe on a weak policy's frozen activations to detect high-risk steps while there is still time to act. When the probe flags a step, control switches to a stronger separate policy, but only for the steps that need it. On LIBERO-Spatial, AEGIS recovers 10.1% of the trajectories the weak policy alone loses, versus 4.6% for budget-matched blind escalation and 5.1% for a random-trigger placebo. These gains are significant under one-sided exact paired McNemar tests with Holm-Bonferroni adjustment over three pre-registered contrasts: +5.4pp over blind escalation, p=8.5e-6; +5.0pp over random triggering, p=1.0e-4; paired-trajectory bootstrap CIs exclude zero. AEGIS activates the stronger policy on only 38% of steps, so the lever is timing rather than compute. The probe clears its precondition with an early-window AUROC of 0.764, 95% CI [0.70, 0.84], read from the weak-policy path over the first 30% of trajectory steps before any handoff. We pre-register the full analysis plan, including a conditional recovered-task-rate estimand and explicit kill criteria, and confirm the result on 700 common-random-number episodes per arm, with nA-fail=646.

</details>


### [178] [A Geometric Account of Activation Steering through Angle-Norm Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06735)
*Georgii Aparin,Tatiana Gaintseva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新审视激活 steering 中“隐藏状态范数不携带概念信息”的假设，发现概念主要编码在角度结构中，但范数对干预稳定性和下游效果仍很关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性激活 steering 简单有效，但加性干预会同时改变隐藏状态方向与范数；球面 steering 试图避免范数变化，其依据是范数可能不包含概念相关信息。本文旨在通过受控实验区分角向与径向成分的作用，检验这一假设。

Method: 作者在七个语言模型上进行经验研究，将 steering 干预分解为两个几何效应：token 隐藏状态与概念方向的角度对齐变化，以及隐藏状态范数变化；比较不同 steering 方法如何耦合这两类效应及其对概念控制和下游行为的影响。

Result: 实验表明，概念表征主要存在于角度结构中，这支持球面方法的基本动机；但隐藏状态范数并非无关，它会影响 steering 的稳定性以及下游生成行为。不同方法即使在概念层面效果相似，也可能因范数变化不同而表现出差异。

Conclusion: 激活 steering 不应只用单一加性系数参数化，因为该系数混合了角向与径向影响；更合理的做法是使用可解释的角度与径向成分分别描述和控制干预。

Abstract: Linear activation steering has gained popularity as a simple and empirically effective way to control language model behavior. More recently, spherical steering paradigms have been proposed to address limitations of additive interventions, often motivated by the assumption that hidden-state norm does not carry concept-relevant information. In this work, we revisit this assumption through a controlled empirical study designed to disentangle the roles of angular and radial components. We show that steering methods differ mainly in how they couple two geometric effects: changing a token's angular alignment with a concept direction and changing its hidden-state norm. Across seven language models, we find that concepts are represented primarily in angular structure, supporting the motivation for spherical methods, but that norm remains important for the stability and downstream effects of steering. Our results explain why interventions with similar concept-level effects can behave differently, and suggest that activation steering should be parameterized by interpretable angular and radial components of the intervention, rather than by a single additive coefficient that entangles these two effects.

</details>


### [179] [OpenSkill: Open-World Self-Evolution for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06741)
*Zhiling Yan,Dingjie Song,Hanrong Zhang,Wei Liang,Yuxuan Zhang,Yutong Dai,Lifang He,Philip S. Yu,Ran Xu,Xiang Li,Lichao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 OpenSkill，用于无目标任务监督的开放世界自进化智能体，通过从公开资源中构建知识、技能与验证信号，实现部署后的自主适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化智能体方法通常依赖可用的学习闭环，例如人工整理的技能、成功轨迹或外部验证器信号；但真实开放世界部署中，智能体可能只有任务提示，缺乏监督、答案或可靠反馈。因此需要研究如何在无目标任务监督条件下自行构建技能与验证机制。

Method: OpenSkill 从文档、代码仓库和网络等开放资源中获取有依据的知识与验证锚点，将其合成为可迁移技能，并基于这些锚点构造虚拟任务进行练习与技能 refinement。其验证过程不依赖目标任务答案，而是使用自建验证器和锚点来形成监督独立的实践环境。

Result: 在三个基准和两个目标智能体上，OpenSkill 在满足无监督约束的前提下取得最佳自动通过率。分析表明，其学习到的技能可跨模型迁移，无需针对特定模型适配；其自建验证器虽未访问真实答案，但与真实结果具有较好一致性。

Conclusion: OpenSkill 证明开放世界资源不仅可作为知识来源，也可作为构建独立练习与验证环境的基础，为无监督条件下的智能体自进化提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents requires adaptation after deployment, but existing approaches assume a usable learning loop, such as curated skills, successful trajectories, or verifier signals. Real open-world deployments may provide none of these, offering only a task prompt. In this work, we study open-world self-evolution, where an agent must build both its skills and its own verification signals from scratch, using open-world resources but no target-task supervision. We propose OpenSkill, a framework that bootstraps this loop: it acquires grounded knowledge and verification anchors from documentation, repositories, and the web, synthesizes them into transferable skills, and refines those skills against self-built virtual tasks grounded in the anchors rather than in target answers. The open world thus supplies both the knowledge to be learned and a supervision-independent practice environment, with target-task supervision reserved for final evaluation. Across three benchmarks and two target agents, OpenSkill attains the best automated pass rate while satisfying the no-supervision constraint. Analysis shows its skills transfer across models without model-specific adaptation, and its self-built verifier aligns with ground-truth outcomes despite never accessing them.

</details>


### [180] [AdMem: Advanced Memory for Task-solving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06787)
*Runzhe Wang,Huilin Lu,Shengjie Liu,Li Dong,Jason Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向大语言模型智能体的统一自动记忆框架，整合语义、情景与程序记忆，并通过短期/长期双层存储和多智能体协作提升长程任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM工具型智能体在长程任务中难以持续记忆、组织和复用知识；已有记忆方法多偏向事实存储，程序记忆方法又常依赖成功轨迹回放，缺少对失败案例的利用与在线可扩展管理。

Method: 设计双层记忆架构，将短期记忆与长期记忆结合，并统一建模语义记忆、情景记忆和程序记忆；引入actor、memory、critic多智能体架构，实现自动记忆生成、奖励标注与自适应检索；长期记忆通过基于奖励的评估、合并与剪枝进行维护。

Result: 在多种环境中的实验表明，该方法相比现有基线在长多轮任务上具有更高成功率与鲁棒性，并能支持持续改进和可扩展记忆管理。

Conclusion: 全面且自适应的记忆机制对于提升LLM智能体在复杂长程任务中的可靠性、知识复用能力和长期性能具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise as tool-using agents but remain limited in long-horizon tasks that require remembering, organizing, and reusing knowledge. Prior memory approaches aim to resolve the situation, but mainly focus on storing factual information. Recent work on procedural memory improves task reuse, yet often reduces to replaying past successes without addressing failure cases or online scalability. We introduce a unified and automatic memory framework that integrates semantic, episodic, and procedural memory in a bi-level design combining short-term and long-term stores. A multi-agent architecture with actor, memory, and critic agents enables automatic memory generation, reward annotation, and adaptive retrieval. Long-term memory is managed through reward-based evaluation, merging, and pruning, ensuring scalability and continual improvement. Experiments across various environments show that our approach improves robustness and success on long multi-turn tasks compared to existing baselines. This work highlights the importance of comprehensive, adaptive memory for advancing LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [181] [Evidence-Based Intelligent Diagnostic and Therapeutic Visualization System with Large Language Models: Multi-Turn Interaction and Multimodal Treatment Plan Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06869)
*Yunhan Wang,Yuda Wang,Zhiying Tu,Mingqiang Song,Li Song,Kun Li,Dianhui Chu,Bolin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种知识增强的可视化中医辅助诊断系统，通过知识图谱、主动问诊和多模态治疗方案展示，提高辨证论治的透明度、可解释性与用户信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助中医诊断工具存在推理过程不透明、交互被动、治疗方案呈现有限等问题，影响临床可信度、患者理解和教学应用。

Method: 系统基于包含241种证候、1,263个症状和2,485条关系的Neo4j知识图谱；采用精确匹配、语义匹配、模糊匹配和大语言模型验证的四阶段症状匹配流程；使用信息增益驱动并经遗传算法优化的主动提问策略；结合AI生成插图、三维经络腧穴模型和循证文献进行多模态治疗展示。

Result: 知识图谱约束使非标准输出减少32%；案例研究验证了其在患者自评、医师辅助诊断和中医教育中的有效性；30例自动配对比较显示诊断信任显著提升（Cohen's d=1.82，p<0.001），认知负荷在五个维度中四个得到改善，循证参考可信度评分提高（4.21 vs. 2.95）。

Conclusion: 该系统通过知识图谱驱动的可视化和多模态交互，增强了中医诊断推理透明度和治疗方案可解释性，为可信AI辅助中医应用提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Aim: Existing AI-assisted traditional Chinese medicine diagnostic tools suffer from opaque reasoning processes, passive interaction, and limited treatment plan presentation. This study proposes a knowledge-enhanced visual diagnostic system to improve the transparency and interpretability of syndrome differentiation and treatment. Methods: The system is built upon a Neo4j knowledge graph comprising 241 syndromes, 1,263 symptoms, and 2,485 relations. It incorporates a four-stage symptom matching pipeline (exact, semantic, fuzzy, and large language model verification), an information gain-driven proactive questioning strategy optimized with genetic algorithms, and a multimodal treatment presentation integrating artificial intelligence-generated illustrations, three-dimensional meridian-acupoint models, and evidence-based literature. Results: Knowledge graph constraints reduced non-standard outputs by 32%. Case studies validated the effectiveness of the interactive workflow across patient self-assessment, clinician-assisted diagnosis, and traditional Chinese medicine education. Automated paired-comparison evaluation across 30 cases further demonstrated significant improvements in diagnostic trust (Cohen's d = 1.82, p < 0.001), reduced cognitive load (improvements in four of five dimensions), and higher credibility of evidence-based references (4.21 vs. 2.95). Conclusions: The proposed system enhances the transparency of traditional Chinese medicine diagnostic reasoning and the interpretability of treatment plans through knowledge graph-driven visualization and multimodal interaction, offering a practical solution for trustworthy artificial intelligence-assisted traditional Chinese medicine applications.

</details>


### [182] [Workflow-to-Skill: Skill Creation via Routing-Workflow-Semantics-Attachments Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06893)
*Yuyang Zhang,Xinyuan Han,Xudong Jiang,Run Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出从多源交互轨迹自动构建大语言模型智能体的技能，强调轨迹应被视为可执行运行时规范而非普通文本摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量技能依赖人工编写，成本高；现有摘要或提示方法难以处理轨迹中的碎片化、冗余以及罕见但关键的安全行为缺失问题。

Method: 提出RWSA工作流式中间表示，将技能分解为Workflow结构、执行Semantics和运行时Attachments；进一步提出W2S框架，对轨迹进行分段、生成局部技能草稿、对齐共享结构、协调分支并压缩冗余，同时保留证据与置信度标注。

Result: 在70个技能上的实验表明，W2S相较基于摘要和提示的基线方法，将行为重放一致性提升了10.5%。

Conclusion: 自动技能构建需要面向工作流和执行语义建模，不能仅将交互轨迹压缩为文本摘要；RWSA与W2S为从异构运行证据中构建可靠技能提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on Skills to encode procedural knowledge, yet high-quality Skills remain costly to hand-write. This paper studies automatic Skill construction from heterogeneous interaction evidence, including demonstrations, agent trajectories, tool traces, and execution logs. We argue that trace-to-skill construction is not simple summarization tasks, because traces are fragmented, redundant, and may miss rare but safety-critical behaviors. To address this, we introduce RWSA, a workflow-oriented intermediate representation that decomposes Skills into Workflow structure, execution Semantics, and runtime Attachments, capturing task decomposition, control flow, verification, safety, rollback, and state management. Building on RWSA, we propose W2S, a framework that segments traces, induces local Skill drafts, aligns shared structures, reconciles branches, and compresses redundancy while preserving evidence and confidence annotations. Experiments on 70 Skills show that W2S improves behavioral replay consistency by 10.5% over summarization- and prompting-based baselines, highlighting the need to treat traces as executable runtime specifications rather than compressible text.

</details>


### [183] [Declarative Skills for AI Agents in Knowledge-Grounded Tool-Use Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06923)
*M. Danish Lim,I. Danial Bin Sharudin,Wen Han Chen,Cedric Lim,Laura Wynter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了声明式、命令式和无脚手架三类工具使用型客服智能体编排机制，发现检索质量是主要瓶颈；高质量检索下，声明式技能文件更能提升流程类任务准确率并减少编排错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实客服工作流依赖非结构化知识库和工具调用，智能体需要有效的编排机制来决定何时检索、何时执行流程、如何遵守任务约束。现有方法包括自然语言技能提示和显式状态机，但二者在真实场景中的效果、鲁棒性与理论差异尚不清晰。

Method: 论文构建并比较三种策略类：读取三个领域技能文件并自主决定控制流的 DeclarativeAgent、基于显式阶段化程序状态机的 ImperativeAgent、以及类似 τ-Knowledge 基准的无脚手架基线。作者将其形式化为去中心化部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程中的策略类，分析信息论和结构性质，并在五种语言模型与两种检索设置上进行实证评估。

Result: 实验表明，检索质量是影响所有智能体表现的主导因素；当证据不完整或偏斜时，三类智能体性能都明显下降，技能文件无法弥补缺失信息。在高质量检索下，声明式技能能稳定提升程序性任务准确率并降低编排错误，而命令式状态机因脆弱性并未可靠提升任务成功率或合规性。

Conclusion: 声明式技能文件是一种较有效且相对稳健的智能体编排范式，尤其适用于检索质量较高的客服流程任务；但系统整体性能首先受制于知识检索质量。相比之下，显式命令式状态机虽然可提供外部控制结构，却可能因僵化和脆弱性而收益有限。

Abstract: We study orchestration mechanisms for tool-using AI agents in realistic customer-service workflows over an unstructured knowledge base. We argue that declarative agents -- AI agents equipped with natural-language skill files appended to the system prompt -- are an effective orchestration paradigm. Concretely, we compare (i) a DeclarativeAgent that reads three domain-specific skill files at inference time and decides its own control flow, (ii) an ImperativeAgent based on a programmatic state machine with explicit phases, and (iii) an unscaffolded baseline agent modeled after the $τ$-Knowledge benchmark agent. Our ImperativeAgent is motivated by externalised-control inference as in Recursive Language Models and graph-based orchestration frameworks. We formalise the three agents as policy classes within a decentralised partially-observable Markov decision process and analyse their information-theoretic and structural properties; we then test the predicted differences empirically on five language models and two retrieval regimes. Our results show that retrieval quality is a dominant bottleneck for AI agents: when evidence is incomplete or skewed, all agents degrade substantially, and skill files cannot recover lost performance. Under high-quality retrieval, however, declarative skills consistently improve accuracy on procedural tasks and reduce orchestration errors, while the imperative state machine's brittleness does not reliably improve task success or compliance.

</details>


### [184] [Quantum-Inspired Trace-Augmented Evidence Selection for Reasoning over Structured Hypothesis Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06941)
*Laura Wynter,Nirvik Sahoo,Paul Griffin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 EP-HUBO，将多条链式思维中的推理片段显式建模为证据选择的高阶二进制优化问题，用于在法律等证据密集型任务中改进答案聚合，尤其保护少数但证据更强的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数投票只选择最常见答案，无法判断支持证据是否更强；在法律推理等专业领域，错误常来自证据细微差异、证据使用不一致和噪声多数压倒正确少数。因此需要一种以证据质量为核心的推理聚合机制。

Method: 方法 EP-HUBO 使用小型本地模型生成多条 CoT 轨迹，将推理片段解析并按候选假设构建证据池；基于相关性、具体性、区分性等质量权重，为每个证据池建立高阶无约束二进制优化问题，并用经典模拟退火或 Dirac-3 光子熵量子机器求解；最后将优化后的证据交由前沿模型进行一次裁决。

Result: 在两个证据密集型法律推理基准上评估，结果表明 HUBO 式优化能够更有原则地聚合推理片段，并保留少数但正确、证据充分的假设；该方法在低污染、前沿模型尚未充分吸收测试材料的领域更有价值。

Conclusion: EP-HUBO 将 CoT 聚合从简单答案频率统计转变为证据质量驱动的组合优化问题，有望提升专业、证据密集型任务中的推理鲁棒性，但其收益取决于证据解析质量、优化权重设计以及基准是否存在数据污染。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) now solve a wide range of expert-level exams at or above human level, yet remain brittle on specialised, evidence-intensive domains such as law. On these tasks, errors arise not only from gaps in world knowledge but also from subtle distinctions between pieces of evidence and inconsistent use of supporting evidence. The most common aggregator over sampled chain-of-thought (CoT) traces, majority vote, returns the most popular answer regardless of whether its evidence is actually strongest. We propose to treat the selection of CoT reasoning fragments into a set of evidence as an explicit combinatorial optimisation problem, allowing well-supported but minority hypotheses to override noisy majorities, and to evaluate the approach on legal-reasoning benchmarks that are particularly sensitive to evidence quality. We introduce EP-HUBO (Evidence Pool Higher-Order Binary Optimisation), which generates multiple CoT traces with a small local model, parses fragments into per-hypothesis evidence pools, solves a higher-order unconstrained binary optimisation per pool with quality-derived weights (relevance, specificity, distinctiveness), and delegates a single adjudication call per question to a frontier model. We evaluate EP-HUBO on two evidence-intensive legal benchmarks using both simulated annealing on classical hardware and the Dirac-3 photonic entropy-quantum machine from Quantum Computing Inc. HUBO-style optimisation gives a principled way to aggregate reasoning fragments while preserving minority-but-correct hypotheses, and is most valuable in low-contamination domains where frontier models have not already absorbed the benchmark material.

</details>


### [185] [Accounting for Context: Shaping Moral Credences for Value Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06972)
*Jazon Szabo,Sanjay Modgil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究智能体在道德不确定性下如何决策，指出在聚合不同道德理论评价时必须纳入情境因素，否则可能出现类似辛普森悖论的反直觉结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有道德不确定性研究试图公平、民主地聚合不同道德理论对行动的评价，但往往忽略现实决策中的情境限制。例如，某些道德视角依赖对行动后果的准确预测，而现实中这种能力常常不足。因此，需要建立能同时处理道德多元性与情境因素的决策框架。

Method: 论文形式化了智能体在道德不确定性下的决策问题，并将影响不同道德理论适用性或评价可靠性的情境因素纳入聚合过程。通过理论分析，考察传统聚合机制在该设定下是否满足弱帕累托原则等常识性性质。

Result: 研究发现，当考虑情境因素后，看似合理的弱帕累托原则可能被违反。该现象并非简单的聚合错误，而是与辛普森悖论相似：忽略分组或背景变量会导致整体判断与局部判断之间出现反转。

Conclusion: 论文认为，道德评价聚合机制若忽视情境因素，可能产生误导性或反直觉的决策结果。智能体对齐人类价值时，不仅要处理道德理论之间的多元性，还必须显式建模行动评估所依赖的现实背景与信息条件。

Abstract: Ensuring that agent behaviours are aligned with human moral values inevitably raises the problem of how to account for the plurality of moral perspectives that societies -- and even individuals -- typically adopt. Work on moral uncertainty proposes mechanisms to fairly and democratically aggregate evaluations of actions across different moral theories. However, this paper argues that one needs to account for contextual factors when aggregating moral evaluations. For example, consequentialist perspectives assume an ability to accurately determine how an agent's actions change the world; an assumption that often does not hold in real world settings. We, therefore, formalise agent decision making under moral uncertainty, while also accounting for these kinds of contextual factors. We thereby show that a seemingly commonsensical property -- the weak Pareto principle -- is violated. We argue that this apparent problem is, in fact, a variation of Simpson's paradox, and hence reveals the limitations of aggregation mechanisms that ignore the impact of contextual factors.

</details>


### [186] [Exploring Agentic Tool-Calling Decisions via Uncertainty-Aligned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06976)
*Yijin Zhou,Linqian Zeng,Xiaoya Lu,Wenyuan Xie,Dongrui Liu,Junchi Yan,Jing Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TRUST，将不确定性量化纳入奖励设计，并结合轻量级关键轮次标注，用于改进 LLM 智能体在多轮工具使用中的决策质量与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 工具使用智能体容易出现不支持的工具调用、幻觉式直接回答等次优决策，并在多步交互中累积错误。已有方法多依赖推理时纠错或基于结果/清单的粗粒度奖励，缺少对决策不确定性特征的利用。作者观察到，面向决策的强化学习会削弱正确与错误动作之间的不确定性区分，导致模型对错误决策过度自信、探索信号变弱。

Method: 提出 TRUST：在奖励设计中引入不确定性量化，将其作为一种“排斥力”以维持正确动作与错误动作之间的不确定性分离；同时使用轻量级关键轮次标注，对多轮轨迹进行统一后训练，从而优化工具调用和直接回答等决策行为。

Result: 在多种工具使用基准上，TRUST 稳定提升了智能体的决策质量和整体任务表现，并在优化过程中保持了更可靠的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，不确定性不仅可用于评估模型可靠性，也可作为强化学习奖励设计的重要信号。通过显式维护正确与错误动作的不确定性分离，TRUST 能缓解过度自信错误并提升多轮工具使用智能体的稳健性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents often make suboptimal tool-use decisions, including unsupported tool invocation and hallucinated direct responses, which may accumulate errors throughout multi-step interactions. Existing approaches mainly improve these behaviors through inference-time correction or coarse-grained reward signals based on decision outcomes and structured checklists, leaving the uncertainty characteristics of agent decisions underexplored. We observe that decision-oriented reinforcement learning tends to weaken the uncertainty separation between correct and incorrect actions, resulting in overconfident mistakes and weaker exploration signals. Therefore, we propose TRUST, which incorporates uncertainty quantification into reward design as a repulsive force for maintaining uncertainty separation, and labels lightweight key-turn annotations for unified post-training of multi-turn trajectories. Experimental results across diverse tool-use benchmarks show that TRUST consistently enhances both decision quality and agent performance while maintaining more reliable uncertainty estimates during optimization.

</details>


### [187] [Teaching the Way, Not the Answer: Privileged Tutoring Distillation for Multimodal Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07000)
*Shizhe Xiang,Ke An,Wenlong Yu,Yue Liu,Jian Luan,Pei Fu,Qilong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 PTD-PO，用“特权提示”在不向学生策略暴露答案的情况下，为 RLVR 提供稠密的逐步 token 分布监督，提升大型视觉语言模型的复杂多模态推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RLVR 依赖稀疏可验证奖励，失败 rollout 缺少 token 级监督，导致复杂多模态推理中的探索效率低；外部教师蒸馏开销高，而答案条件调优可能泄露答案信息并诱发捷径式生成。

Method: 构建 Privileged Tutoring Distillation Policy Optimization（PTD-PO）框架：利用空间注意力引导和中间文本推理步骤生成结构化特权提示，通过上下文学习产生逐步 token 分布监督；学生策略仍在无答案原始上下文中优化，并将失败 rollout 与带提示参考模型进行 token 分布对齐；同时引入 Top-K Jensen-Shannon divergence 以缓解 guided/unguided 上下文分布偏移并降低显存开销。

Result: 在 2B 到 8B 参数规模的 LVLM 上，PTD-PO 稳定优于 RLVR 及多种蒸馏基线，缓解熵坍塌，并提升复杂多模态推理表现。

Conclusion: PTD-PO 证明了在不暴露答案且无需高成本外部教师的条件下，利用结构化特权提示进行稠密蒸馏可有效增强 RLVR 训练效率与 LVLM 推理能力。

Abstract: Recent post-training methods, particularly Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), have significantly enhanced the reasoning ability of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs). However, the sparse nature of verifiable rewards provides little token-level supervision for failed rollouts, often leading to inefficient exploration in complex multimodal reasoning tasks.
